Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the innate structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 387. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 968 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on innate structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 387. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 968. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^940(S, F) is trivial. QED.
